Theorem 211.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on number_theory structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 48. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 203 with relations in degree 1.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to number_theory theory, we decompose the problem into 48 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 203. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(1/n). QED.
